- V1248: a scan pass that read any root only in part (unreadable entries in its walk) never ends `completed`: it ends Partial, or Failed if every root failed, and counts that root among the folders that could not be read. Guarded by pharos-server `scan_tasks::tests::a_root_read_in_part_makes_the_pass_partial` and pharos-scanner `fs::tests::incomplete_walk_does_not_prune_transiently_unreadable_files` (asserts `walk_errors`).
- V1249: a full scan never sweeps a row because its file failed to probe: a failed re-probe of an existing row, and a probe-memo skip of a file that already has a row, stamp the row seen with its stored signature, count `pharos_scan_kept_unreadable_total{reason}` (`reprobe_failed`, `known_bad`) and log the row and path. Guarded by pharos-scanner `fs::tests::a_row_whose_file_cannot_be_re_read_is_kept`.
- V1250: a music queue kept for the tab is shown only to the user it was built for. Entering sign-in by any road empties it, a saved record with songs and no owner is not read, and a restored queue whose owner is not the signed-in user is emptied before use. Guarded by pharos-ui `native::queue::tests::a_saved_queue_is_only_shown_to_the_user_it_was_built_for`.
- V1251: no song of a queue read back after a reload shows before the signed-in user is known: it is held apart, adopted only when built for that user and nothing was queued meanwhile, and dropped otherwise or on sign-in. Guarded by pharos-ui `native::queue::tests::a_held_queue_is_adopted_only_by_its_own_user`.
- V1208: a pointer drag on the video seek bar (mouse, pen or touch) previews its target and issues exactly one seek, to where the pointer let go; a cancelled pointer issues none; the moves before the release seek nothing; the end is logged as `pharos: scrub released|cancelled input=<mouse|pen|touch|remote> from … ms to … ms after N moves|presses in … ms`. Guarded by pharos-ui `watch_view::tests::a_drag_on_the_seek_bar_seeks_once_when_it_lets_go` and `scrub::tests::a_drag_previews_until_it_lets_go_and_logs_its_input`.
- V1209: the video seek bar is focusable in every build (`tabindex="0"`, `role="slider"`); a TV build makes it a D-pad stop too (V1300, B1330). Guarded by pharos-ui `watch_view::tests::the_seek_bar_marks_passed_chapters_and_the_play_head` render and the T038-300 browser check.
- V1210: the seek bar draws the buffered range ahead of the play head and nothing when none is ahead. Guarded by pharos-ui `watch_view::tests::the_seek_bar_shows_what_is_buffered_ahead_of_the_play_head`.
- V1211: the player's menus and window-size buttons sit in `.pharos-control-menus`, which is `display: contents` on a wide screen and a sheet behind the settings button under 40rem; speed and subtitle delay sit behind the settings button on every screen. Guarded by pharos-ui `watch_view::tests::the_menus_wait_behind_a_settings_button_the_sheet_opens`.
- V1212: a touch double tap on the left or right third of the picture within 300 ms seeks 10 s back or forward and does not also play or pause; the middle third does not seek. Guarded by pharos-ui `watch_view::tests::a_double_tap_on_a_side_third_seeks_that_way`.
- V1213: the video player's wheel does nothing in the compact view and for pixel travel under half a notch; line and page travel and notches, whole or scaled by browser zoom, step the volume. Guarded by pharos-ui `watch_view::tests::the_video_wheel_leaves_a_trackpad_to_the_page`.
- V1214: the video player renders no text or emoji glyph for its transport, back, fullscreen or mute buttons. Guarded by pharos-ui `watch_view::tests::the_player_draws_its_glyphs_as_icons_not_text`.
- V1215: the compact player's progress line has an id to measure and its fill lets the pointer through, so a click on it seeks to that fraction. Guarded by pharos-ui `stage::tests::the_small_players_progress_line_is_a_seek_target`.
- V1281: a set of a film's part files (V1150's naming) whose name less the part marker has a whole copy elsewhere in the library is never an item, a stack or a version: the whole file is the film, each part's earlier row folds its viewers' state into it and is swept, and the decision counts `pharos_scan_part_stack_total{outcome="whole_copy"}` and logs the whole file. A part set with no whole copy still stacks. The fold goes to the item the whole file is grouped under, covers the parts' edition files, and is skipped when the parts name different films. Guarded by pharos-scanner `fs::tests::a_film_held_whole_is_not_stacked_beside_itself`, `fs::tests::a_stack_folds_into_the_whole_film_item_among_its_copies`, `fs::tests::a_hidden_part_folds_into_its_whole_films_item`, `fs::tests::parts_beside_their_whole_film_are_hidden` and `parts::tests::a_parts_whole_film_is_its_name_less_the_marker`.
- V988: the Android TV app plays a direct stream (a song) on its media element straight from the server, carrying only the Media-scoped token (ADR-0058) as `api_key`, never the device token, and asks for the next media token before the current one expires (`MEDIA_TOKEN_MARGIN_MS` ahead, never sooner than `MEDIA_TOKEN_MIN_WAIT_MS` after the last ask). The element's `error` reaches the player as a failure carrying its code and message (B1029). Guarded by pharos-ui `tv_page` `a_direct_stream_loads_from_the_server_with_the_media_token`, `the_next_media_token_is_asked_for_before_this_one_expires` and `a_media_error_reads_from_the_bridge`.
- V989: the Android TV app's page bridge passes a song's `timeupdate` on to Rust at least four times as often as a video's (`AUDIO_TICK_MS` 250 ms, `VIDEO_TICK_MS` 1000 ms), so the karaoke line and wipe follow the song as closely as in a browser (B1030). Guarded by pharos-ui `tv_page` `a_song_ticks_four_times_as_often_as_a_video`.
- V992: on the Android TV app, the music visualiser runs in the page as pharos-ui's own wasm loop, one run per mounted `Visualiser`, stopped when it unmounts. A run stopped before its start script arrives never starts. A run that cannot draw reports its `render::Fallback` label back, so the screen falls back to its CSS look. A song's element is fetched with CORS so the analyser hears it, and `audio_tap` never logs a source's query (B1032, ADR-0104). Guarded by pharos-ui `tv_page` `a_page_visualiser_starts_stops_and_skips_a_stopped_run` and `render` `a_fallback_reads_back_from_its_label`.
- V993: on the Android TV app, the lyrics panel centres the line being sung, as in a browser: `center_child` scrolls the panel in the page so the child's middle sits at the panel's middle, never above the top (B1033). Guarded by pharos-ui `tv_page` `a_lyrics_line_is_centred_in_its_panel`.
- V994: on the Android TV app, the full music screen is no focus trap: Up or Down from its section moves the focus to its play button, the arrows then move between its controls (Lyrics, Karaoke, Queue, the visualiser picker, the transport), skipping a control scrolled out of its panel and every lyrics line, and Up with nothing above returns to the section, where Left and Right seek. The music screen in the mini bar or floating window is not taken (B1034). Guarded by pharos-ui `tv_page` `the_remote_moves_round_the_full_music_screen` and `lyrics` `the_tv_remote_skips_name_the_lyrics_lines`.
- V995: with the player docked in the mini bar, Down from the page's lowest controls reaches the bar and Up from the bar reaches the page, even where the fixed bar lies over the rows at the page's foot (B1035). Guarded by pharos-ui `keymap` `down_reaches_a_bar_laid_over_the_last_row` and `the_docked_bar_is_reached_last_and_left_by_up`.
- V996: the Android TV app's page reports each visualiser run's outcome itself (`started`, `stopped_first`, or the fallback label with its error); the app never reads a lost eval result as a fallback, and only a fallback changes the music screen (B1036). Guarded by pharos-ui `tv_page` `a_page_visualiser_starts_stops_and_skips_a_stopped_run`; on the TV, `visualiser page run=N outcome=started` follows each `action=start`.
- V997: a media element is routed through Web Audio once and keeps that routing for its life; a later tap reuses it and a stopped visualiser leaves it, so a style change neither throws nor silences the element (B1037). Checked on the TV by `audio tap outcome=reused` after a style change, with no `InvalidStateError`.
- V998: choosing a style in the visualiser picker closes the picker and returns focus to its button (B1038). Checked on the TV: OK on a tile logs `visualiser picker chosen=<style> shown=false` and the grid is gone from the next screenshot.
- V1177: on a TV (`data-device="tv"`, 960 x 540) the home page shows at least two whole rails of eight or more posters, the library grid at least eight columns, and no poster text is under 12 CSS px (B1217). Checked by screenshot at 960 x 540.
- V1178: a visualiser run whose median frame interval over 60 drawn frames exceeds 20 ms draws at 0.8x its pixel ratio per such window, never below 0.5x and never rising within the run; pauses and gaps over 250 ms do not count (B1218, ADR-0111). Guarded by pharos-ui `render::resolution` tests.
- V1179: the WebGL visualiser reads its canvas's CSS box at most every 30 frames (B1219). Checked on the TV by the trace's layout time during karaoke.
- V1180: on the TV the karaoke line glides without a filter; elsewhere it keeps its glow (B1220). Guarded by pharos-ui `lyrics` `the_tv_glides_the_karaoke_fill_without_the_glow`.
- V1182: with a player docked in the TV's mini bar, Up from a page's top control reaches the bar, and the Info key goes to the bar and back from anywhere (B1222). Guarded by pharos-ui `keymap` `up_from_the_top_of_the_page_reaches_the_docked_bar` and the `tv_page` bridge test.
- V1280: on the Android TV app every page script, including those from root-scope tasks, runs against the window's `Document`, never the no-op fallback: after start the root carries `data-device="tv"` and the key bridge stays installed (B1310). Checked on the TV: one `pharos: bridge installed` and no `bridge ended` or `eval failed` in the start log.
- V1173: speaker colours are opt-in and change nothing when off. Only a plain, non-typeset line whose speaker has a palette slot is drawn in a speaker style; a script without speaker names, or a request without `speakers=1`, compiles to the same bytes as without the feature, under a cache name with no speaker suffix (B1213). Guarded by pharos-styling `speakers::tests`, `recurring_speakers_get_slots_in_the_plan`, `speaker_slots_colour_plain_lines_only_when_the_theme_has_speakers`, `speaker_styles_are_dialogue_in_distinct_colours`, `ass_name_field_is_the_speaker`; pharos-cache `styled_names_differ_per_field`; pharos-server `speaker_colours_are_a_switch_of_their_own`; pharos-ui `a_styled_track_is_fetched_at_the_tier_and_level`.
- V1196: a series page opens on the season holding the viewer's next episode: the part-watched last played episode, else the first unwatched episode after the most recently played one (season 0 only after a special), looking at most `SEASON_WALK` later regular seasons; nothing played opens the first season numbered above 0; the hero's Play starts that episode while its season is shown; the page states the verdict in `data-season-pick` (asked, first-regular, first, no-seasons, next-up, resume, unplaced, finished, walk-limit), labels distinct. Guarded by `browse::tests::a_series_opens_on_the_season_after_a_finished_one`, `a_series_opens_on_the_season_being_watched`, `play_starts_the_episode_after_the_last_played_not_a_skipped_one`, `a_finished_series_opens_on_the_last_season_watched`, `a_series_with_nothing_watched_opens_on_season_one`, `season_pick_labels_are_distinct`
- V1197: below 40rem an episode row gives its name a line of its own (two lines at most) and every control in it is at least 44px; the queue buttons sit behind one More control there, which is drawn only when the queue buttons are. Guarded by `browse::tests::an_episode_row_folds_its_queue_buttons_behind_more` (markup) and a 390px static render (layout)
- V1198: a music library's tab and album order round-trip through its URL query (Artists in name order is the bare URL; an unknown value opens the default); its tabs are a single tab stop that the arrow keys, Home and End step along, wrapping. Guarded by `browse::tests::a_music_library_keeps_its_tab_and_album_order_in_the_url`, `music_tabs_step_by_arrow_key`, `a_music_library_has_keyed_tabs`
- V1199: What's New never says a pick was saved when storing it failed: the failure and its cause replace the saved line until a later pick is kept. Guarded by `whats_new::tests::a_pick_that_was_not_kept_is_not_called_saved`
- V1200: while Home or a title page loads, the copy last shown stands in, if one was kept; a page left by a link and returned to by Back or Forward is scrolled to where it was left, and a page reached by a link opens at its top. The kept copies are bounded (one Home, `revisit::TITLES` titles, `revisit::PLACES` positions). Guarded by `app::tests::home_draws_the_last_home_shown_while_it_loads`, `revisit::tests::recent_keeps_the_newest_value_per_key_up_to_its_cap`, `revisit::tests::a_place_is_put_back_only_on_a_return`
- V1201: the search page's term round-trips through `?q=`; its matches are grouped by kind in a fixed order with each group in the server's order; when fewer matches are shown than the total, the page says how many of how many. Guarded by `find::tests::hits_group_by_kind_in_a_fixed_order_keeping_match_order`, `the_search_term_round_trips_through_the_url`, `the_cap_is_stated_only_when_matches_are_left_out`, `views::tests::the_search_page_groups_matches_and_states_the_cap`
- V1202: on web, the arrow keys reach every enabled select on the page as they reach buttons and links, and an arrow pressed on a select moves the focus rather than changing the select's value. Not covered by a host test (web.rs is wasm only); check in a browser
- V1203: every Manage page round-trips through `?page=` on `/manage`, Users being the bare URL and an unknown value opening Users. Guarded by `manage::tests::a_manage_page_round_trips_through_the_url`
- V1204: a failed paging step shows its reason beside its retry; an empty Home names who can add a library (Manage for an admin); the shell's library list, trail and account name are asked for a second time before they are given up, and a library list that failed twice is said. Guarded by `browse::tests::a_failed_page_says_why`, `views::tests::an_empty_home_is_worded_for_who_is_looking`, `views::tests::the_shell_says_when_the_libraries_could_not_load`, `paging::tests`
- V1205: a description language pick the server refused is said beside the select with its cause, and the select shows the value still in use. Guarded by `account_settings::tests::a_failed_description_language_save_is_said`, `a_failed_description_language_save_keeps_the_last_confirmed_value`
- V1206: Manage's Remove on a hand-made show fix asks once more (Keep or Remove) before it sends anything. Guarded by `admin::tests::each_entry_offers_its_actions`
- V1207: the palette lists Manage only for an admin, and always lists Together, Playback and Account. Guarded by `palette::tests::the_places_are_home_the_libraries_favourites_then_settings`
- V1293: on a TV (`data-device="tv"`) an album card draws no queue buttons (its status note still shows), so every remote move onto a card lands on the tile, and OK opens the album; elsewhere the card keeps Play next and Add to queue (B1322). Guarded by pharos-ui `browse` `the_tv_draws_no_queue_buttons_on_album_cards`.
- V1230: the Android TV app submits client events through the same opt-in gate as the browser, only while the signed-in user opted in, and each carries `client="android-tv"` exactly once (B1267). Guarded by pharos-ui `tv_page` `a_tv_event_names_its_client_once` and the `diagnostics` gate tests; checked on the TV by `pharos: telemetry installed opted_in=true` in its log and a `client event` line with `android-tv` in Loki.
- V1231: on the Android TV app the page going hidden pauses a playing video and reports the position through the player's hidden hook; a song plays on (B1268). Guarded by the `tv_page` bridge parse test; checked on the TV by `pharos: page visibility hidden=true paused_videos=1` and a `progress report reason=hidden` after Home during a film.
- V1232: a remote's rewind and fast-forward seek in both players (the scrub preview on a TV), and on the TV next and previous reach the track-skip hook and Stop pauses and leaves a video (B1269). Guarded by pharos-ui `keymap` `a_remotes_rewind_and_fast_forward_seek_in_both_players` and `tv_page` `the_remotes_next_and_previous_keys_skip`.
- V1233: on the TV, OK on the picture presses a shown Skip or Up Next before it pauses, and Back drops a scrub preview, then cancels Up Next, then leaves (B1270). Guarded by pharos-ui `watch_view` `back_drops_a_preview_then_cancels_up_next_then_leaves` and `tv_page` `ok_presses_the_buttons_the_player_draws`.
- V1234: on the TV, Back closes the first shown dialog, menu, search list, settings sheet or queue before it changes the page, and never holds Back on an overlay that did not close on Escape (B1271). Guarded by the `tv_page` bridge tests; checked on the TV by `pharos: back closed overlay`.
- V1235: on the TV the banner offers a Search button, not the search box, and opening the palette focuses its input (B1272). Checked on the TV by `pharos: palette opened via=banner` and the remote's keyboard showing.
- V1236: on the TV, coming back to a library grid puts the tile that had the focus back where it sat and focuses it (B1273). Guarded by pharos-ui `tv_page` `a_kept_place_puts_the_tile_back_where_it_was_and_focuses_it`; checked on the TV by `pharos: grid place restored placed=true`.
- V1237: on the TV every enabled input is a D-pad stop; an arrow a field does not keep leaves it, and OK toggles a checkbox (B1274). Guarded by pharos-ui `tv_page` `a_key_says_when_the_focus_is_on_a_field`; checked on the TV by `pharos: keys: field left`.
- V1238: an installed app's out-of-date notice names the server's version, the app's, and the update (B1275). Guarded by pharos-ui `app` `the_apps_outdated_notice_names_both_versions_and_the_update`.
- V1239: a URL's query or fragment never changes its route, and on the TV `current_search` is the query of the app's current path (B1276). Guarded by pharos-ui `route` tests and `tv_page` `a_paths_search_is_its_query`.
- V1290: a section of a fixed-layout EPUB (`rendition:layout` `pre-paginated`, unless its itemref says `rendition:layout-reflowable`; or any itemref saying `rendition:layout-pre-paginated`) with a known page size is drawn as exactly one page of that size, scaled to fit the reader less an 8 px gutter and centred, keeping the book's own font sizes, colours and positions; a viewport in a reflowable book is ignored (B1319). Guarded by quire it_quire `a_pre_paginated_book_gives_each_section_its_page_size`, `a_reflowable_item_in_a_fixed_book_flows`, `a_viewport_in_a_reflowable_book_is_not_a_page`, quire-web `layout` `a_fixed_page_fits_its_frame_and_is_centred`, `settings` `a_fixed_page_keeps_the_book_own_sizes_and_colours`, and the fixed-layout checks in `just quire-web-check`.
- V1291: a book's `@font-face` fonts load only from files inside the book: a rule with no source inside it is dropped, the rule never reaches a stylesheet's text, and `quire-web` adds each face to the document's font set from a `blob:` URL of the book's bytes and deletes it when the view is removed (B1320, ADR-0119; narrows V351's "drops `@font-face`"). Guarded by quire `css::tests` `a_font_inside_the_book_is_handed_out_apart`, `a_font_face_with_no_font_inside_the_book_is_dropped`, `a_descriptor_carrying_syntax_is_dropped`, it_quire `a_fixed_page_keeps_its_placed_text_and_its_font`, and `just quire-web-check` ("the book's font reaches the document").
- V1240: the seek preview is whole inside the seek bar at every target, its centre clamped to half its drawn width from either end, and on a TV the player controls sit inside the 48 x 27 px title-safe area (B1277). Guarded by pharos-ui `watch_view` `the_seek_preview_stays_inside_the_bar_at_either_end` and `on_a_tv_the_controls_keep_to_the_title_safe_area`.
- V1294: the Android TV activity's `configChanges` includes `density`, `keyboard`, `keyboardHidden`, `navigation`, `orientation`, `screenLayout`, `screenSize`, `smallestScreenSize` and `uiMode`, so no configuration change a TV makes recreates it (B1323). Guarded by pharos-ui `tv_page` `the_tv_manifest_keeps_the_activity_through_config_changes`; checked on the TV by attaching a keyboard with the app open.
- V1300: on the TV app the seek bar is a D-pad stop in the video player: Up from the control row reaches it, Left and Right on it move the remote scrub preview (V855) rather than the focus, and OK on it commits an open preview or plays and pauses without moving the focus off it (B1330). Guarded by pharos-ui `tv_page::tests` (`REMOTE_SLIDERS` is `#` + `watch_view::SEEK_ID` and fills the bridge) and the seek bar's `tabindex=0` assertion in `watch_view::tests`.
- V1175: a picture crop keeps the bars above and below whenever a subtitle may sit in them: a burned subtitle (V977) or a text track drawn as the browser's own cues inside the `<video>` (no styled track drawing and the Rust renderer not drawing it); the decision logs `pharos: picture crop item=… rows_kept=burned|cues|no` once per change (B1215). Guarded by pharos-ui `only_the_browsers_own_cues_count_as_drawn_in_the_video`, `the_bars_stay_for_a_burned_subtitle_or_the_browsers_cues`, `a_burned_subtitle_keeps_the_bars_above_and_below`.
- V1302: an APK is signed for the TV only after `scripts/android-apk-check.sh` passes it: label Pharos, versionCode above 1, a leanback launcher entry, the `pharos_banner` banner, the Pharos mark as the adaptive icon's foreground, the manifest's theme `AppTheme` with a Pharos Night window background, and every config change of V1294; `android-apk.sh` stops on a failed dx or gradle step (B1329). Guarded by pharos-ui `tv_page` `the_tv_manifest_names_the_pharos_label_icon_banner_and_theme`, `the_tv_launch_theme_is_pharos_night`, `the_tv_overlay_has_its_icon_and_banner_and_the_build_applies_it`, and the checker's own run in `android-apk.sh`.
- V1303: each fix to the TV remote's script has a browser test that runs the script as the TV does: `tv-remote.spec.ts` installs `BRIDGE_JS`, read from `android_page.rs` and filled by `tv_page::bridge_script`'s own replacements, in the web build at 960 x 540 with the TV's user agent, and fails if a placeholder is left or a web shim no longer matches (B1332). A new remote fix adds its walk there, or a row to `docs/tv-smoke-checklist.md` when it is behind `cfg!(target_os = "android")`.
- V1304: every batch compiles the TV app: `just tv-check` (clippy `-D warnings`, `--features mobile`, `armv7-linux-androideabi`) runs in the `dioxus UI` CI job (B1333).
- V1286: following a book link whose target names an element, by `id` or by an old `<a name>`, shows the page of its section that holds that element, across sections and into non-linear ones; a link with no fragment opens its section at the start (B1315). Guarded by quire `a_named_anchor_is_a_link_target`, `just quire-web-check` and the Dioxus reader spec's links test.
- V1287: in the EPUB reader Up and Down move the focus among the book links drawn on the page shown, in reading order, never to one on another page; Down from the bar enters them; Enter follows the focused link (B1316). Guarded by pharos-ui `reader` `up_and_down_walk_the_links`, `just quire-web-check` and the Dioxus reader spec's links test.
- V1288: following a book link offers "↩ Return" to the place it was followed from until it is used, a contents row is chosen, or another link is followed, which offers Return to the place that link was followed from instead (one step back, not a history) (B1317). Guarded by pharos-ui `reader` `a_link_offers_return_and_contents_does_not` and the Dioxus reader spec's links test.
- V1289: every reader jump logs `pharos: reader jump` with its `via`, `from`, `to` and `landed` (`start`, `target`, `missing_target`, or `place` for a return), and `at`, the place shown after it; a failed one logs `reader jump failed` with `via`, `from`, `to` and the error, and no `landed` (B1318). The lines are the browser console's; a problem report's breadcrumbs carry `reader jump via= landed=` (no book text). Guarded by the Dioxus reader spec's links test.
- V1282: a library's sort and filters take one row on every screen, with no `<select>` (a music library's Albums tab, which has only an order, has the Sort pill alone: V1306): a Sort pill naming the order and direction (`Sort: <order>, <direction>`), a Filters pill naming how many filters are on (`Filters, N on`), a pill per filter on (`Remove filter: <label>`) that clears only that filter, and Reset filters while any choice is not the default. Every choice lives in one `Sort and filter` dialog of pressed/unpressed pills, grouped Sort by, Order, Watched, Genre, 3D and (on a library that groups box sets) Collections, that applies each choice at once (B1311). Guarded by pharos-ui `browse` `a_library_filter_row_is_pills_that_count_and_remove_what_is_on`, `the_filter_sheet_offers_every_choice_as_a_pressed_pill`, `each_filter_on_has_a_pill_and_clearing_it_keeps_the_rest`, and the Dioxus E2E library tests that drive it.
- V1283: the sort and filter sheet is modal (`aria-modal`, the page behind inert), so arrow keys and a remote's D-pad stay inside it; Done, Escape, the remote's Back or a tap outside closes it and the focus returns to the pill that opened it, which focuses the chosen pill of its group as it opens. A focus ring on a control in a sideways-scrolling row (the library tabs) is never clipped: the row leaves room at least as large as the TV ring's reach (B1311, B1312). Guarded by pharos-ui `browse` `the_library_tabs_leave_room_for_the_tv_focus_ring`; checked on the TV by screenshot.
- V1284: a library's own grid shows no heading on screen (its tab is lit and in sight) and keeps it for screen readers; every other grid keeps its visible heading (B1313). Checked by the Dioxus E2E browse test (`section.pharos-browse h2` attached) and by screenshot.
- V1285: from 64rem wide, and on a TV, the top bar is one row (brand, library tabs, search and links), and the page's content starts below that one row; under 64rem off a TV the bar keeps its rows, and under 40rem the brand, Favourites, Together and the account share one row (B1314). Checked by screenshot at 390 x 844, 1366 x 768 and the TV's 960 x 540.
- V1292: on a phone (under 40rem) the A to Z or year rail never overlaps the header's library tabs at the top of the page: it is anchored to the foot of the screen (B1321). Checked by screenshot at 390 x 844.
- V1305: a music library's page shows no heading on screen (its tab is lit in the top bar) and keeps it for screen readers, and its Artists / Albums / Playlists tabs are one row the height of the library filter row, which leaves room for the TV's focus ring and has no scroll box inside it to clip that ring (B1334). Guarded by pharos-ui `browse` `a_music_library_keeps_its_heading_for_readers_and_its_tabs_on_one_row` and `the_library_tabs_leave_room_for_the_tv_focus_ring`; checked by screenshot at 960 x 540 (TV) and 390 x 844.
- V1306: a music library's albums order is one Sort pill on the tab row (`Sort: <order>, <direction>`), never a `<select>`, and it opens the sort and filter dialog with only the Sort by and Order groups, titled Sort, modal as V1283 sets out (B1335). Guarded by pharos-ui `browse` `the_albums_order_is_a_sort_pill_and_a_sort_only_sheet`.
- V1317: while the mini bar shows, the page's root has a non-`auto` `scroll-padding-bottom` taller than the bar, so a control scrolled into view (the TV's arrow keys, `scrollIntoView`) stops above it, and the A to Z rail ends above it; any custom property such a root rule reads carries a fallback, since `.pharos-main`'s variables are not set on `<html>` (B1346). Guarded by `stage::tests::a_tile_scrolled_into_view_stops_above_the_mini_bar` and checked in Chromium at 960 x 540 (a mid-grid tile's foot at 452px, bar top 483px; 540px before).
- V1314: every family button (`.pharos-button`, `.pharos-chip`) has one corner, `calc(var(--px-radius) * 999)`, set in one rule, and no other rule sets a corner on a family button; no rule draws any button with a 4px corner. Sizes come from `--pharos-button-h` / `--pharos-button-h-small` and the matching font variables on `:root` (2.25rem / 1.75rem; 2.75rem / 2.25rem on a coarse pointer; 1.9rem / 1.5rem on a TV) (B1343, ADR 0120). Guarded by pharos-ui `browse` `every_family_button_is_the_one_pill`.
- V1315: an album, film or episode page draws Play next and Add to queue as secondary family buttons; a song row, album card or episode row draws them as bare icons (B1344). Guarded by pharos-ui `browse` `page_queue_buttons_are_family_buttons_and_row_ones_are_not`.
- V1316: a button on a Settings or Manage page is drawn with the family's pill corner, except the nav rows and tiles named in B1345, which keep the theme's corner (B1345). Guarded by V1314's test (no 4px button) and checked by screenshot.
- V1309: on a TV the video player's chrome is two rows at most: the seek bar and one bar of transport, time and the settings button. The track, subtitle size, quality, version and view-mode menus and Small window are never in the bar on a TV; they are in the settings sheet, a panel above the bar inside the title-safe area that the remote enters on its first menu and Back closes (B1338). Guarded by pharos-ui `watch_view` `on_a_tv_the_menus_wait_in_the_settings_sheet`; checked on the TV by photo.
- V1310: the player shows a Fullscreen button only on a platform that can toggle fullscreen; the TV app shows none (B1339). Guarded by the `platform::fullscreen_supported` gate (the test host answers true and `the_window_size_controls_are_grouped_smallest_first` keeps the browser's button).
- V1313: on a TV, the skip intro/recap/credits button and Up Next's Play now take the remote's focus as they appear, from the picture, the seek bar (when not scrubbing) or the control row, never from an open settings sheet, queue or invite panel, and every skip is logged with its reason; when the card goes, the focus is never left on nothing, it returns to the picture (B1342). Guarded by pharos-ui `watch_view` `the_skip_button_is_a_focus_target_for_the_remote` (the ids) and checked on the TV by the logcat line `pharos: card focus to=`.
- V1311: a playable Continue Watching or Next Up tile opens its title page on a press (OK, click, tap) and plays on a hold of `tv_page::TILE_HOLD_MS` (OK held on the TV, a mouse button or finger held elsewhere); a hold never opens the title page as well. The tile draws no control inside its box, since the remote's arrows never reach one there; its play link is never drawn (no arrow or Tab stops on it), and no hint is drawn on it (B1340). Supersedes V1192's "starts playback in one press" and info link. Guarded by pharos-ui `views` `a_resume_tile_opens_the_title_and_plays_on_a_hold`, `a_tile_with_nothing_to_play_opens_its_page_and_has_no_play_link`, `tv_page` `ok_on_a_play_at_once_tile_opens_its_title_and_a_hold_plays`, and `tv-remote.spec.ts` (B1340).
- V1332: a device in a watch-together group can leave it from the Together page without opening the player: the group it is in is marked and offers Leave group in place of Join, a group the list does not show still offers Leave group, and the bar's Together link is marked while the device is in a group (B1361). Guarded by pharos-ui `together_view` `the_group_you_are_in_offers_leave_instead_of_join`, `a_group_missing_from_the_list_can_still_be_left`, `outside_a_group_the_page_offers_no_leave`, and `views` `the_shell_marks_together_while_in_a_group`.
- V1333: a watch-together group nothing has played in for `GROUP_IDLE_MS` (3 h) ends whether or not its members are connected, and every member is told `GroupLeft`; a group that is playing is never ended for idleness, and the count starts when playing stops. The end is counted as `pharos_syncplay_group_dissolved_total{cause="idle"}` (B1362). Guarded by pharos-sync `group` `a_connected_group_nothing_plays_in_ends_after_the_idle_limit`, `a_playing_group_is_not_ended_for_idleness` and `dissolve_causes_are_distinct_and_stable`.
